<p dir="auto">Note:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Important</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>mixtral-8x22B-Instruct-v0.3.tar</code> is exactly the same as <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mixtral-8x22B-Instruct-v0.1" rel="nofollow">Mixtral-8x22B-Instruct-v0.1</a>, only stored in <code>.safetensors</code> format</li>
<li><code>mixtral-8x22B-v0.3.tar</code> is the same as <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mixtral-8x22B-v0.1" rel="nofollow">Mixtral-8x22B-v0.1</a>, but has an extended vocabulary of 32768 tokens.</li>
<li><code>codestral-22B-v0.1.tar</code> has a custom non-commercial license, called <a href="https://mistral.ai/licenses/MNPL-0.1.md" rel="nofollow">Mistral AI Non-Production (MNPL) License</a></li>
<li><code>mistral-large-instruct-2407.tar</code> has a custom non-commercial license, called <a href="https://mistral.ai/licenses/MRL-0.1.md" rel="nofollow">Mistral AI Research (MRL) License</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>All of the listed models above support function calling. For example, Mistral 7B Base/Instruct v3 is a minor update to Mistral 7B Base/Instruct v2,  with the addition of function calling capabilities.</li>
<li>The "coming soon" models will include function calling as well.</li>
<li>You can download the previous versions of our models from our <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/getting-started/open_weight_models/#downloading" rel="nofollow">docs</a>.</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Usage</h3><a id="user-content-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Usage" href="#usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>News!!!</strong>: Mistral Large 2 is out. Read more about its capabilities <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-2407/" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Create a local folder to store models</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export MISTRAL_MODEL=$HOME/mistral_models
mkdir -p $MISTRAL_MODEL"><pre><span class="pl-k">export</span> MISTRAL_MODEL=<span class="pl-smi">$HOME</span>/mistral_models
mkdir -p <span class="pl-smi">$MISTRAL_MODEL</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Download any of the above links and extract the content, <em>e.g.</em>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export 12B_DIR=$MISTRAL_MODEL/12B_Nemo
wget https://models.mistralcdn.com/mistral-nemo-2407/mistral-nemo-instruct-2407.tar
mkdir -p $12B_DIR
tar -xf mistral-nemo-instruct-2407.tar -C $12B_DIR"><pre><span class="pl-k">export</span> 12B_DIR=<span class="pl-smi">$MISTRAL_MODEL</span>/12B_Nemo
wget https://models.mistralcdn.com/mistral-nemo-2407/mistral-nemo-instruct-2407.tar
mkdir -p <span class="pl-smi">$1</span>2B_DIR
tar -xf mistral-nemo-instruct-2407.tar -C <span class="pl-smi">$1</span>2B_DIR</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">or</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export M8x7B_DIR=$MISTRAL_MODEL/8x7b_instruct
wget https://models.mistralcdn.com/mixtral-8x7b-v0-1/Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1-Instruct.tar
mkdir -p $M8x7B_DIR
tar -xf Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1-Instruct.tar -C $M8x7B_DIR"><pre><span class="pl-k">export</span> M8x7B_DIR=<span class="pl-smi">$MISTRAL_MODEL</span>/8x7b_instruct
wget https://models.mistralcdn.com/mixtral-8x7b-v0-1/Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1-Instruct.tar
mkdir -p <span class="pl-smi">$M8x7B_DIR</span>
tar -xf Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1-Instruct.tar -C <span class="pl-smi">$M8x7B_DIR</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For Hugging Face models' weights, here is an example to download <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mistral-Small-3.1-24B-Instruct-2503" rel="nofollow">Mistral Small 3.1 24B Instruct</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from pathlib import Path
from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download


mistral_models_path = Path.home().joinpath(&quot;mistral_models&quot;)

model_path = mistral_models_path / &quot;mistral-small-3.1-instruct&quot;
model_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

repo_id = &quot;mistralai/Mistral-Small-3.1-24B-Instruct-2503&quot;

snapshot_download(
    repo_id=repo_id,
    allow_patterns=[&quot;params.json&quot;, &quot;consolidated.safetensors&quot;, &quot;tekken.json&quot;],
    local_dir=model_path,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">pathlib</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Path</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">huggingface_hub</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">snapshot_download</span>


<span class="pl-s1">mistral_models_path</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Path</span>.<span class="pl-c1">home</span>().<span class="pl-c1">joinpath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"mistral_models"</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">model_path</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">mistral_models_path</span> <span class="pl-c1">/</span> <span class="pl-s">"mistral-small-3.1-instruct"</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model_path</span>.<span class="pl-c1">mkdir</span>(<span class="pl-s1">parents</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>, <span class="pl-s1">exist_ok</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">repo_id</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"mistralai/Mistral-Small-3.1-24B-Instruct-2503"</span>

<span class="pl-en">snapshot_download</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">repo_id</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">repo_id</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">allow_patterns</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">"params.json"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"consolidated.safetensors"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"tekken.json"</span>],
    <span class="pl-s1">local_dir</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">model_path</span>,
)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Usage</h2><a id="user-content-usage-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Usage" href="#usage-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following sections give an overview of how to run the model from the Command-line interface (CLI) or directly within Python.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI</h3><a id="user-content-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI" href="#cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Demo</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To test that a model works in your setup, you can run the <code>mistral-demo</code> command.
<em>E.g.</em> the 12B Mistral-Nemo model can be tested on a single GPU as follows:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mistral-demo $12B_DIR"><pre>mistral-demo <span class="pl-smi">$1</span>2B_DIR</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Large models, such <strong>8x7B</strong> and <strong>8x22B</strong> have to be run in a multi-GPU setup.
For these models, you can use the following command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="torchrun --nproc-per-node 2 --no-python mistral-demo $M8x7B_DIR"><pre>torchrun --nproc-per-node 2 --no-python mistral-demo <span class="pl-smi">$M8x7B_DIR</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>Note</em>: Change <code>--nproc-per-node</code> to more GPUs if available.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Chat</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To interactively chat with the models, you can make use of the <code>mistral-chat</code> command.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mistral-chat $12B_DIR --instruct --max_tokens 1024 --temperature 0.35"><pre>mistral-chat <span class="pl-smi">$1</span>2B_DIR --instruct --max_tokens 1024 --temperature 0.35</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For large models, you can make use of <code>torchrun</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="torchrun --nproc-per-node 2 --no-python mistral-chat $M8x7B_DIR --instruct"><pre>torchrun --nproc-per-node 2 --no-python mistral-chat <span class="pl-smi">$M8x7B_DIR</span> --instruct</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>Note</em>: Change <code>--nproc-per-node</code> to more GPUs if necessary (<em>e.g.</em> for 8x22B).</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Chat with Codestral</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To use <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/codestral/" rel="nofollow">Codestral</a> as a coding assistant you can run the following command using <code>mistral-chat</code>.
Make sure <code>$M22B_CODESTRAL</code> is set to a valid path to the downloaded codestral folder, e.g. <code>$HOME/mistral_models/Codestral-22B-v0.1</code></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mistral-chat $M22B_CODESTRAL --instruct --max_tokens 256"><pre>mistral-chat <span class="pl-smi">$M22B_CODESTRAL</span> --instruct --max_tokens 256</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you prompt it with <em>"Write me a function that computes fibonacci in Rust"</em>, the model should generate something along the following lines:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Sure, here's a simple implementation of a function that computes the Fibonacci sequence in Rust. This function takes an integer `n` as an argument and returns the `n`th Fibonacci number.

fn fibonacci(n: u32) -&gt; u32 {
    match n {
        0 =&gt; 0,
        1 =&gt; 1,
        _ =&gt; fibonacci(n - 1) + fibonacci(n - 2),
    }
}

fn main() {
    let n = 10;
    println!(&quot;The {}th Fibonacci number is: {}&quot;, n, fibonacci(n));
}

This function uses recursion to calculate the Fibonacci number. However, it's not the most efficient solution because it performs a lot of redundant calculations. A more efficient solution would use a loop to iteratively calculate the Fibonacci numbers."><pre>Sure, here<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>s a simple implementation of a function that computes the Fibonacci sequence in Rust. This function takes an integer `n` as an argument and returns the `n`th Fibonacci number.</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">fn fibonacci(n: u32) -&gt; u32 {</span>
<span class="pl-s">    match n {</span>
<span class="pl-s">        0 =&gt; 0,</span>
<span class="pl-s">        1 =&gt; 1,</span>
<span class="pl-s">        _ =&gt; fibonacci(n - 1) + fibonacci(n - 2),</span>
<span class="pl-s">    }</span>
<span class="pl-s">}</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">fn main() {</span>
<span class="pl-s">    let n = 10;</span>
<span class="pl-s">    println!("The {}th Fibonacci number is: {}", n, fibonacci(n));</span>
<span class="pl-s">}</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s">This function uses recursion to calculate the Fibonacci number. However, it<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>s not the most efficient solution because it performs a lot of redundant calculations. A more efficient solution would use a loop to iteratively calculate the Fibonacci numbers.</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can continue chatting afterwards, <em>e.g.</em> with <em>"Translate it to Python"</em>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Chat with Codestral-Mamba</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To use <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/codestral-mamba/" rel="nofollow">Codestral-Mamba</a> as a coding assistant you can run the following command using <code>mistral-chat</code>.
Make sure <code>$7B_CODESTRAL_MAMBA</code> is set to a valid path to the downloaded codestral-mamba folder, e.g. <code>$HOME/mistral_models/mamba-codestral-7B-v0.1</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">You then need to additionally install the following packages:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install packaging mamba-ssm causal-conv1d transformers"><pre class="notranslate"><code>pip install packaging mamba-ssm causal-conv1d transformers
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">before you can start chatting:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mistral-chat $7B_CODESTRAL_MAMBA --instruct --max_tokens 256"><pre>mistral-chat <span class="pl-smi">$7</span>B_CODESTRAL_MAMBA --instruct --max_tokens 256</pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Chat with Mathstral</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To use <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mathstral/" rel="nofollow">Mathstral</a> as an assistant you can run the following command using <code>mistral-chat</code>.
Make sure <code>$7B_MATHSTRAL</code> is set to a valid path to the downloaded codestral folder, e.g. <code>$HOME/mistral_models/mathstral-7B-v0.1</code></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mistral-chat $7B_MATHSTRAL --instruct --max_tokens 256"><pre>mistral-chat <span class="pl-smi">$7</span>B_MATHSTRAL --instruct --max_tokens 256</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you prompt it with <em>"Albert likes to surf every week. Each surfing session lasts for 4 hours and costs $20 per hour. How much would Albert spend in 5 weeks?"</em>, the model should answer with the correct calculation.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can then continue chatting afterwards, <em>e.g.</em> with <em>"How much would he spend in a year?"</em>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Chat with Mistral Small 3.1 24B Instruct</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To use <a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-small-3-1/" rel="nofollow">Mistral Small 3.1 24B Instruct</a> as an assistant you can run the following command using <code>mistral-chat</code>.
Make sure <code>$MISTRAL_SMALL_3_1_INSTRUCT</code> is set to a valid path to the downloaded mistral small folder, e.g. <code>$HOME/mistral_models/mistral-small-3.1-instruct</code></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="    mistral-chat $MISTRAL_SMALL_3_1_INSTRUCT --instruct --max_tokens 256"><pre>    mistral-chat <span class="pl-smi">$MISTRAL_SMALL_3_1_INSTRUCT</span> --instruct --max_tokens 256</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you prompt it with <em>"The above image presents an image of which park ? Please give the hints to identify the park."</em> with the following image URL <em><a href="https://huggingface.co/datasets/patrickvonplaten/random_img/resolve/main/yosemite.png" rel="nofollow">https://huggingface.co/datasets/patrickvonplaten/random_img/resolve/main/yosemite.png</a></em>, the model should answer with the Yosemite park and give hints to identify it.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can then continue chatting afterwards, <em>e.g.</em> with <em>"What is the name of the lake in the image?"</em>. The model should respond that it is not a lake but a river.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Python</h3><a id="user-content-python" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Python" href="#python"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><em>Instruction Following</em>:</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from mistral_inference.transformer import Transformer
from mistral_inference.generate import generate

from mistral_common.tokens.tokenizers.mistral import MistralTokenizer
from mistral_common.protocol.instruct.messages import UserMessage
from mistral_common.protocol.instruct.request import ChatCompletionRequest


tokenizer = MistralTokenizer.from_file(&quot;./mistral-nemo-instruct-v0.1/tekken.json&quot;)  # change to extracted tokenizer file
model = Transformer.from_folder(&quot;./mistral-nemo-instruct-v0.1&quot;)  # change to extracted model dir

prompt = &quot;How expensive would it be to ask a window cleaner to clean all windows in Paris. Make a reasonable guess in US Dollar.&quot;

completion_request = ChatCompletionRequest(messages=[UserMessage(content=prompt)])

tokens = tokenizer.encode_chat_completion(completion_request).tokens

out_tokens, _ = generate([tokens], model, max_tokens=1024, temperature=0.35, eos_id=tokenizer.instruct_tokenizer.tokenizer.eos_id)
result = tokenizer.instruct_tokenizer.tokenizer.decode(out_tokens[0])

print(result)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">mistral_inference</span>.<span class="pl-s1">transformer</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Transformer</span>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from pathlib import Path

from mistral_inference.generate import generate
from mistral_inference.transformer import Transformer

model_path = Path.home().joinpath(&quot;mistral_models&quot;) / &quot;mistral-small-3.1-instruct&quot; # change to extracted model

tokenizer = MistralTokenizer.from_file(model_path / &quot;tekken.json&quot;)

    [tokens],
    model,

)

<ul dir="auto">
<li><em>Fill-in-the-middle (FIM)</em>:</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Make sure to have <code>mistral-common &gt;= 1.2.0</code> installed:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install --upgrade mistral-common"><pre class="notranslate"><code>pip install --upgrade mistral-common
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can simulate a code completion in-filling as follows.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from mistral_inference.transformer import Transformer
from mistral_inference.generate import generate
from mistral_common.tokens.tokenizers.mistral import MistralTokenizer
from mistral_common.tokens.instruct.request import FIMRequest

tokenizer = MistralTokenizer.from_model(&quot;codestral-22b&quot;)
model = Transformer.from_folder(&quot;./mistral_22b_codestral&quot;)

prefix = &quot;&quot;&quot;def add(&quot;&quot;&quot;
suffix = &quot;&quot;&quot;    return sum&quot;&quot;&quot;

request = FIMRequest(prompt=prefix, suffix=suffix)

tokens = tokenizer.encode_fim(request).tokens

out_tokens, _ = generate([tokens], model, max_tokens=256, temperature=0.0, eos_id=tokenizer.instruct_tokenizer.tokenizer.eos_id)
result = tokenizer.decode(out_tokens[0])

middle = result.split(suffix)[0].strip()
print(middle)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">mistral_inference</span>.<span class="pl-s1">transformer</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Transformer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">mistral_inference</span>.<span class="pl-s1">generate</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">generate</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">mistral_common</span>.<span class="pl-s1">tokens</span>.<span class="pl-s1">tokenizers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">mistral</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">MistralTokenizer</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">mistral_common</span>.<span class="pl-s1">tokens</span>.<span class="pl-s1">instruct</span>.<span class="pl-s1">request</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">FIMRequest</span>

<span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">MistralTokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_model</span>(<span class="pl-s">"codestral-22b"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Transformer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_folder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"./mistral_22b_codestral"</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">prefix</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"""def add("""</span>
<span class="pl-s1">suffix</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"""    return sum"""</span>

<span class="pl-s1">request</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">FIMRequest</span>(<span class="pl-s1">prompt</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">prefix</span>, <span class="pl-s1">suffix</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">suffix</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">tokens</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">encode_fim</span>(<span class="pl-s1">request</span>).<span class="pl-c1">tokens</span>

<span class="pl-s1">out_tokens</span>, <span class="pl-s1">_</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">generate</span>([<span class="pl-s1">tokens</span>], <span class="pl-s1">model</span>, <span class="pl-s1">max_tokens</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">256</span>, <span class="pl-s1">temperature</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.0</span>, <span class="pl-s1">eos_id</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">instruct_tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">eos_id</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">result</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">decode</span>(<span class="pl-s1">out_tokens</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>])

<span class="pl-s1">middle</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">result</span>.<span class="pl-c1">split</span>(<span class="pl-s1">suffix</span>)[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>].<span class="pl-c1">strip</span>()
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">middle</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Test</h3><a id="user-content-test" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Test" href="#test"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To run logits equivalence:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python -m pytest tests"><pre class="notranslate"><code>python -m pytest tests
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deployment</h2><a id="user-content-deployment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deployment" href="#deployment"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>deploy</code> folder contains code to build a <a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm">vLLM</a> image with the required dependencies to serve the Mistral AI model. In the image, the <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/">transformers</a> library is used instead of the reference implementation. To build it:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker build deploy --build-arg MAX_JOBS=8"><pre>docker build deploy --build-arg MAX_JOBS=8</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Instructions to run the image can be found in the <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/quickstart" rel="nofollow">official documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model platforms</h2><a id="user-content-model-platforms" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model platforms" href="#model-platforms"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
